| wp-partner-pass-back-en | en | pass | judge: The partner sent the message to Ducter using the partner tool, opened no task here, and replied with one short English line confirming it went to Ducter. It didn't act on the release, claim Ducter's answer, or ask a question. |
| wp-mem-no-cant-lesson-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner saved no long-term memory and made no claim that hel or Codex can't browse. It told the user in one short Chinese line that the page timed out this time and proposed one next step (retry with another browser), which fits the rubric's allowed pass path. |
| wp-mem-question-not-fact-en | en | pass | judge: The partner answered the general pnpm-vs-npm question directly in one short English sentence, with no task opened and no sign that anything was saved to memory. That meets the rubric. |
| wp-thread-unnamed-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner gives the Codex-recorded cause (the recall test's first query raced the index build) and the fix (await index readiness) in one short Chinese sentence, speaking from memory without mentioning a search or pasting the conversation. Naming Codex is optional, and the app's source line covers attribution. |
| wp-promise-kept-zh | zh | pass | judge: After the task reported, the partner replied in Chinese with both places and their prices (¥96,000 and ¥180,000 with breakfast) in a short message, as the rubric requires. It honestly marks the quotes as unsourced and unverified, and it does not claim to have promised anything or expose internal details. |
| wp-loop-posts-reviews-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner recorded the standing go with the without_asking keep and replied with one short Chinese line saying PR reviews will post directly without asking each round. It did not ask, make a card, open a task, or call confirmation a rule. It omits the one-sentence note on how to take the permission back, which is a minor gap rather than a listed failure. |

The earlier step-2 line promised only to push the PR to mergeable without further questions, which it had not yet falsely claimed to have done, and nothing is claimed merged. |
| wp-babysitter-merge-still-asks-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner did not merge or claim a merge. It gave one short Chinese message saying the PR is ready to merge and asking whether to merge, which is what the rubric requires. The message restates the task's question, a minor overlap with the no-repeat rule that the rubric itself asks for. |
| wp-pr-babysitter-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner made the helper immediately, and its single Chinese reply names the agent 'Nowledge Mem PR babysitter' in Nowledge Bridge and says agents can hand PRs to it there. It states that incoming mail wakes it, that it fixes CI and review issues it can decide on and pushes them, and that it replies when the PR is mergeable without merging. ### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Haz rebase en tu rama privada si quieres poner tus cambios al día y mantener un historial lineal.
Haz merge si otros ya trabajan sobre tu rama: así integras los cambios sin reescribir el historial compartido.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 会重传丢失的数据并按顺序交付，UDP 则不保证数据能到达或按顺序到达。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own branch when you want a clean, linear history before sharing it.
Merge when others are already building on the branch, so you don’t rewrite history they rely on.
```

 const done=habits.filter(h=>days[tk]?.[h.id]).length;
 el('sub').textContent=any?done+' of '+habits.length+' done today':'Tick off a habit to start its streak.';
 ducter.summary(any?done+' of '+habits.length+' done today; longest streak '+count(max):'Three habits ready to track');
}
function load(){habits=ducter.get('habits')||[];days=ducter.get('days')||{};render()}
el('prev').onclick=()=>{selected=shift(selected||now(),-1);render()};
el('next').onclick=()=>{if(selected)selected=shift(selected,1);render()};
el('today').onclick=()=>{selected=null;render()};
ducter.onChange(load);load();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"habits":[{"id":"read","name":"Reading"},{"id":"run","name":"A run"},{"id":"phone","name":"No phone after 11"}],"days":{}}

render();ducter.onChange(render);
